######## 7. The seed check script

==== The seed check script: seed_check.py (sha256 4e7fe47e412ff48c8ae3d919d6cdb1c6d5dec510001580ae6ce54653a895cae8, 4633 bytes) ====
"""(U2a VERIFICATION, seeds 5681-5684) My own seed check: are 5681-5684 unused anywhere?

    py -3.12 seed_check.py [--fault]
(1) every ledger.json (worktree, main checkout, the bot tuner's clone READ ONLY): any entry whose [first, last] overlaps
    5681-5684, of ANY family;
(2) a text search for the four numbers as whole tokens (not inside a longer digit run) across ai_player/ and
    PROJECT KNOWLEDGE/ of the worktree and the main checkout, plus every earlier stage folder of s53/ (rows of earlier
    rounds carry the seeds they ran on);
--fault: the same search with 5671-5674 (seeds the fix round USED), which MUST report hits, proving the search can see
    a used range (a check that cannot fail proves nothing).
"""
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

FAULT = "--fault" in sys.argv
FIRST, LAST = (5671, 5674) if FAULT else (5681, 5684)
W = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_6a7c527a-7d2-1")
M = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement")
LEARNER = Path("C:/Remote Projects/pokebot-learner")
S53 = Path("C:/Users/Ripto/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Remote-Projects-Pokebot-Gen3-Enhancement/"
           "2cb1657d-9e66-4a34-afd4-cbf84eb4a4a8/scratchpad/s53")
ME = S53 / "u2" / "build" / "verify-rerun"

ledger_hits = []
for root in (W, M, LEARNER):
    path = root / "ai_player" / "gates" / "ledger.json"
    if not path.exists():
        print(f"ledger {path}: absent")
        continue
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    entries = data["entries"] if isinstance(data, dict) else data
    for e in entries:
        if e.get("first") is None:
            continue
        if not (e["last"] < FIRST or e["first"] > LAST):
            ledger_hits.append((str(path), e["id"], e["family"], e["first"], e["last"], e["status"]))
    print(f"ledger {path}: {len(entries)} entries")
for hit in ledger_hits:
    print("  LEDGER OVERLAP", hit)

# v2: a whole token (no letter/digit on either side, not a line reference "file:NNNN"), and counted as a SEED use only
# in a seed context: "seed" within 80 characters before it, a row/arm file name "-NNNN.json", a ledger "first"/"last"
# key, or a range "NNNN-NNNN" whose other end is also in 5600-5699. v1 (raw digits) hit hashes and line numbers.
nums = "|".join(str(s) for s in range(FIRST, LAST + 1))
pattern = re.compile(r"(?<![0-9A-Za-z:.])(" + nums + r")(?![0-9A-Za-z])")


def seed_context(text: str, m: re.Match) -> bool:
    before = text[max(0, m.start() - 80):m.start()].lower()
    after = text[m.end():m.end() + 8]
    if "seed" in before:
        return True
    if after.startswith(".json") and text[m.start() - 1:m.start()] == "-":
        return True
    if re.search(r'"(first|last)":\s*$', before):
        return True
    if re.match(r"\s*[-–]\s*56\d\d(?!\d)", after) or re.search(r"56\d\d\s*[-–]\s*$", before):
        return True
    return False


text_hits = []
scanned = 0
roots = [W / "ai_player", W / "PROJECT KNOWLEDGE", M / "ai_player", M / "PROJECT KNOWLEDGE", S53]
for root in roots:
    for folder, dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        if Path(folder).resolve().is_relative_to(ME.resolve()):
            continue
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ("__pycache__", "profiles", "buildroots", "trees", "frozen")]
        for name in files:
            if not name.endswith((".py", ".json", ".jsonl", ".txt", ".md", ".out", ".log")):
                continue
            path = Path(folder) / name
            try:
                if path.stat().st_size > 30_000_000:
                    continue
                text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            except OSError:
                continue
            scanned += 1
            for m in pattern.finditer(text):
                if seed_context(text, m):
                    text_hits.append((str(path), m.group(1),
                                      text[max(0, m.start() - 60):m.end() + 40].replace("\n", " ")))
            if pattern.search(path.name):
                text_hits.append((str(path), "file name", path.name))
files = sorted({h[0] for h in text_hits})
print(f"text files scanned: {scanned}; hits: {len(text_hits)} in {len(files)} files")
for f in files[:25]:
    sample = next(h for h in text_hits if h[0] == f)
    print(f"  HIT {f}: ...{sample[2]}...")
used = bool(ledger_hits or text_hits)
label = "FAULT (5671-5674, MUST be used)" if FAULT else "5681-5684"
print(f"SEEDS {label} USED ANYWHERE: {len(ledger_hits) + len(text_hits)} -> "
      f"{'USED' if used else 'UNUSED: YES'}")
sys.exit(0 if (used if FAULT else not used) else 1)

######## 15. The plan script

==== The plan script: run_plan.py (sha256 9bc42922a4ce5fa5712adc8631893839c80e09622f21c9d3c1570ef0369ac441, 5903 bytes) ====
"""(U2a VERIFICATION, seeds 5681-5684) ONE plan of EVERY live_r1 arm, run from the worktree, each job through runcmd.py
(its command, start/end time and exit code saved under OUT/logs/), dependencies respected, at most N at once (12).

    py -3.12 run_plan.py OUT [N]

Per seed, 20 jobs (80 in all), the same arm set #63, #64 and #65 ran:
  CONTROL, K-AFTER, K-INSIDE, K-SILENT, K-FIRST                     no dependency
  K-BETWEEN (rule new), K-BETWEEN-MID (midpoint, diagnostic)         after CONTROL
  K-INSIDE-OLD, K-SILENT-OLD                                         after their twin
  LAUNCH, LAUNCH-OLD                                                 after K-AFTER
  CHAIN, CHAIN-OLD, CHAIN-R1-OLD                                     after K-SILENT
  R2-RETRY                                                           after K-AFTER
  R2-RETRY-OLD                                                       after R2-RETRY
  U2-BUDGET, U2-BUDGET-OLD                                           after LAUNCH
  U2-WALL                                                            after K-AFTER
  U2-WALL-OLD                                                        after U2-WALL
Bounded: runcmd's per-job timeout (1 hour, far above every arm's own timeouts) and the whole plan abandoned past 3 hours,
children killed by PID (only processes this plan started).
"""
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
W = "C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_6a7c527a-7d2-1"
BR = "C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/sandbox-pokebot"
SEEDS = range(5681, 5685)
LIVE = "ai_player/gates/live_r1.py"


def jobs_for(out: Path) -> list[tuple[str, list[str], list[str]]]:
    rows = out / "rows"
    row = lambda name, seed: str(rows / f"{name}-{seed}.json")  # noqa: E731
    jobs = []
    for s in SEEDS:
        jobs.append((f"CONTROL-{s}", ["control", "--seed", str(s)], []))
        for arm in ("K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST"):
            jobs.append((f"{arm}-{s}", ["arm", "--arm", arm, "--seed", str(s)], []))
    for s in SEEDS:
        jobs.append((f"K-BETWEEN-{s}", ["arm", "--arm", "K-BETWEEN", "--seed", str(s), "--between-from",
                                        row("CONTROL", s), "--between-rule", "new"], [f"CONTROL-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"K-BETWEEN-MID-{s}", ["arm", "--arm", "K-BETWEEN", "--tag", "K-BETWEEN-MID", "--seed", str(s),
                                            "--between-from", row("CONTROL", s), "--between-rule", "midpoint"],
                     [f"CONTROL-{s}"]))
        for twin in ("K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT"):
            jobs.append((f"{twin}-OLD-{s}", ["old", "--twin-row", row(twin, s)], [f"{twin}-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"LAUNCH-{s}", ["launch", "--from-row", row("K-AFTER", s)], [f"K-AFTER-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"LAUNCH-OLD-{s}", ["launch", "--from-row", row("K-AFTER", s), "--old"], [f"K-AFTER-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"CHAIN-{s}", ["chain", "--from-row", row("K-SILENT", s)], [f"K-SILENT-{s}"]))
        for old in ("CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD"):
            jobs.append((f"{old}-{s}", ["chain", "--old", old, "--from-row", row("K-SILENT", s)], [f"K-SILENT-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"R2-RETRY-{s}", ["r2", "--from-row", row("K-AFTER", s)], [f"K-AFTER-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"R2-RETRY-OLD-{s}", ["r2", "--old", "--from-row", row("R2-RETRY", s)], [f"R2-RETRY-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"U2-BUDGET-{s}", ["u2budget", "--from-row", row("LAUNCH", s)], [f"LAUNCH-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"U2-BUDGET-OLD-{s}", ["u2budget", "--from-row", row("LAUNCH", s), "--old"], [f"LAUNCH-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"U2-WALL-{s}", ["u2wall", "--from-row", row("K-AFTER", s)], [f"K-AFTER-{s}"]))
        jobs.append((f"U2-WALL-OLD-{s}", ["u2wall", "--old", "--from-row", row("U2-WALL", s)], [f"U2-WALL-{s}"]))
    return jobs


def stamp() -> str:
    return time.strftime("%I:%M:%S %p")


def main() -> int:
    out = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
    limit = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 12
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    jobs = jobs_for(out)
    assert len(jobs) == 80 and len({j[0] for j in jobs}) == 80, "the plan must be 80 distinct jobs"
    pending = {j[0]: j for j in jobs}
    running: dict[str, subprocess.Popen] = {}
    done: dict[str, int] = {}
    began = time.monotonic()
    print(f"PLAN VERIFY: {len(jobs)} jobs, at most {limit} at once, tree {W}, out {out} ({stamp()})", flush=True)
    while pending or running:
        if time.monotonic() - began > 3 * 3600:
            print(f"PLAN ABANDONED: over 3 hours ({stamp()})", flush=True)
            for proc in running.values():
                proc.kill()
            return 9
        for jid, proc in list(running.items()):
            code = proc.poll()
            if code is not None:
                done[jid] = code
                del running[jid]
                print(f"  done {jid}: exit {code} ({stamp()})", flush=True)
        ready = [j for j in pending.values() if all(d in done for d in j[2])]
        for jid, args, _ in ready[:max(0, limit - len(running))]:
            del pending[jid]
            cmd = [sys.executable, str(HERE / "runcmd.py"), str(out / "logs" / f"{jid}.out"), "--cwd", W,
                   "--timeout", "3600", "--env", f"AI_PLAYER_BUILD_ROOT={BR}", "--env", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1",
                   "--env", "PYTHONUTF8=1", "--", "py", "-3.12", "-B", LIVE, *args, "--out", str(out)]
            running[jid] = subprocess.Popen(cmd)
            print(f"  start {jid} ({stamp()})", flush=True)
        time.sleep(2)
    bad = {k: v for k, v in done.items() if v != 0}
    print(f"PLAN DONE: {len(done)} jobs, exit codes not 0: {bad or 'none'} ({stamp()}, "
          f"{time.monotonic() - began:.0f} s)", flush=True)
    return 1 if bad else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

######## 20. The verifier's instrument: my_checks.py

==== The verifier's instrument: my_checks.py: my_checks.py (sha256 ea1dca41a8ff0113edb1885ce0ceb0589c232613b548170a694a27e1fa0b7d56, 34609 bytes) ====
"""(U2a VERIFICATION, seeds 5681-5684) MY OWN instrument: every discriminator pair and the U2 arms' checks, recomputed
from the PRIMARY files of a live_r1 plan's OUT folder (carts, launches.jsonl, purity.json, memory.jsonl, world.jsonl,
actions.jsonl, the children's RESULT lines), never from the driver's grade. It imports nothing of ai_player or live_r1;
carts are read by my_sav.py (my own parser). The driver's rows are read for exactly two things, each named where used:
the SaveWatch RAM truth of each save (`a.saves[*].truth`, the harness's record, not the AI's) and, for CHAIN's pair,
the driver's `counted` flag (CHAIN's rule-N reach is NOT recomputed here).

    py -3.12 my_checks.py OUT FIRST LAST [--fault NAME | --all-faults] [--json FILE]
--fault NAME plants one fault in the LOADED data (never on disk) and the run must then show a check that was True turn
False (exit 0 when it does). --all-faults runs the clean pass then every fault; exit 0 only if the clean pass has no
False U2 check and every fault flips at least one check.
"""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import my_sav  # noqa: E402

W = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_6a7c527a-7d2-1")
BUDGET = 2620
COST = re.compile(r"cost=[0-9.]+ms")

# U0's two edits, copied by hand from the spec's mutant table (the U2-BUDGET-OLD / U2-WALL-OLD tree must be the
# worktree's launcher.py with EXACTLY these two edits).
U0_EDITS = [
    ("        allowed = kind == \"INVOLUNTARY\" or kind == \"BUDGET\" or at_save\n",
     "        allowed = kind == \"INVOLUNTARY\" or at_save  # MUTANT U0\n"),
    ("    if kind == \"BUDGET\" and clean is not None:\n",
     "    if kind == \"BUDGET\" and False:  # MUTANT U0\n"),
]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- readers
def jl(path) -> list:
    out = []
    try:
        text = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        return out
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        try:
            out.append(json.loads(raw))
        except ValueError:
            continue  # a kill can cut the last line short
    return out


def result(path) -> dict | None:
    try:
        for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("RESULT "):
                return json.loads(line[7:])
    except OSError:
        return None
    return None


def sha(path) -> str | None:
    try:
        return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    except OSError:
        return None


def row(out, name, seed) -> dict:
    try:
        return json.loads((out / "rows" / f"{name}-{seed}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except OSError:
        return {}


def truths(out, seed, arm) -> list:
    """SaveWatch RAM truth per save, from the driver's row (the harness's record): [(counter, truth)]"""
    return [(s["counter"], s["truth"]) for s in row(out, arm, seed).get("a", {}).get("saves", []) if s.get("truth")]


def sessions(mem):
    return [l for l in mem if l.get("kind") == "session"]


def save_by_identity(mem, truth) -> list:
    """save lines whose stamp (H:MM), tile and party PIDs equal a RAM truth (identity, never position)"""
    pids = list(truth["pids"])
    hits = []
    for l in mem:
        if l.get("kind") != "save":
            continue
        if list(l.get("play_time") or []) != list(truth["play_time"][:2]):
            continue
        if list((l.get("where") or {}).get("tile") or []) != list(truth["pos"]):
            continue
        if [p["pid"] for p in l.get("party") or []] != pids:
            continue
        hits.append(l["id"])
    return hits


def last_snapshot(path) -> dict | None:
    lines = jl(path)
    return lines[-1] if lines and isinstance(lines[-1], dict) else None


def key_to_const(snapshot) -> dict:
    return {k: v.get("const") for k, v in (snapshot or {}).get("maps", {}).items()}


def new_maps_after(world_path, frame) -> list:
    snap = last_snapshot(world_path)
    if snap is None:
        return None
    return sorted(k for k, v in snap.get("maps", {}).items() if isinstance(v.get("first_frame"), int)
                  and v["first_frame"] > frame)


def continue_pressed(res) -> bool:
    for p in res.get("presses") or []:
        if p[1] == "A" and isinstance(p[2], list) and p[2][0] == "Task_HandleMainMenuInput" and p[2][2] == 0:
            return True
    return False


def first_look(actions_path, frame_base):
    for l in jl(actions_path):
        obs = l.get("observation") or {}
        if l.get("frame") is not None:
            return [obs.get("screen", {}).get("game_state"), l["frame"] - frame_base]
    return None


def rebuild_last_save_id(mem, save_id):
    """follow the save's parent chain to its root, apply each line's memory delta (set/del) to a goals tree, read
    goals.last_save.id (my own rebuild; only the 'goals' subtree is applied)"""
    saves = {l["id"]: l for l in mem if l.get("kind") == "save"}
    chain, at = [], save_id
    while at is not None and at in saves:
        chain.append(saves[at])
        at = saves[at].get("parent")
    state: dict = {}
    for line in reversed(chain):
        delta = line.get("memory") or {}
        if line.get("full") or "set" not in delta:  # a full save line holds the whole memory, not a delta
            state = {"goals": copy.deepcopy(delta.get("goals") or {})}
            continue
        for path in delta.get("del") or []:
            node = state
            for part in path[:-1]:
                node = node.get(part, {}) if isinstance(node, dict) else {}
            if isinstance(node, dict):
                node.pop(path[-1], None)
        for path, value in delta.get("set") or []:
            if not path or path[0] != "goals":
                continue
            node = state
            for part in path[:-1]:
                node = node.setdefault(part, {})
                if not isinstance(node, dict):
                    break
            else:
                node[path[-1]] = copy.deepcopy(value)
    ls = state.get("goals", {}).get("last_save")
    return ls.get("id") if isinstance(ls, dict) else None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- per-seed load
def load(out: Path, seed: int) -> dict:
    fr, rn, pr = out / "frozen", out / "runs", out / "profiles"
    d = {"seed": seed}
    d["truth_kafter"] = truths(out, seed, "K-AFTER")
    d["truth_control"] = truths(out, seed, "CONTROL")
    for arm in ("K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST", "K-BETWEEN", "K-BETWEEN-MID"):
        d[f"file:{arm}"] = my_sav.parse(fr / f"{arm}-{seed}" / "current_save.sav") \
            if (fr / f"{arm}-{seed}" / "current_save.sav").exists() else None
        d[f"amem:{arm}"] = jl(fr / f"{arm}-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
        d[f"aworld:{arm}"] = rn / f"{arm}-{seed}" / "a_run" / "world.jsonl"
        d[f"aactions:{arm}"] = rn / f"{arm}-{seed}" / "a_run" / "actions.jsonl"
        d[f"bmem:{arm}"] = jl(pr / f"ai-r1live-{arm.lower()}-b-{seed}" / "ai_player" / "memory.jsonl")
    d["file:CONTROL"] = my_sav.parse(pr / f"ai-r1live-control-{seed}" / "current_save.sav")
    d["aactions:CONTROL"] = rn / f"CONTROL-{seed}" / "a_run" / "actions.jsonl"
    for twin in ("K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT"):
        d[f"bmem:{twin}-OLD"] = jl(pr / f"ai-r1live-{twin.lower()}-old-b-{seed}" / "ai_player" / "memory.jsonl")
    for name in ("LAUNCH", "LAUNCH-OLD"):
        d[f"lmem:{name}"] = jl(fr / f"{name}-after-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
        d[f"llaunch:{name}"] = jl(fr / f"{name}-after-{seed}" / "launches.jsonl")
        d[f"lactions:{name}"] = rn / f"{name}-{seed}" / "launch_run" / "actions.jsonl"
        d[f"lrun:{name}"] = rn / f"{name}-{seed}" / "launch_run"
    d["lpurity"] = json.loads((rn / f"LAUNCH-{seed}" / "launch_run" / "purity.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) \
        if (rn / f"LAUNCH-{seed}" / "launch_run" / "purity.json").exists() else None
    d["lcart_sha"] = sha(fr / f"LAUNCH-after-{seed}" / "current_save.sav")
    d["kafter_cart_sha"] = sha(fr / f"K-AFTER-{seed}" / "current_save.sav")
    d["lmem_sha"] = sha(fr / f"LAUNCH-after-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
    for name in ("U2-BUDGET", "U2-BUDGET-OLD", "U2-WALL", "U2-WALL-OLD"):
        d[f"res:{name}"] = result(rn / f"{name}-{seed}" / "child_c.out")
        prof = pr / f"ai-r1live-{name.lower()}-c-{seed}" / "ai_player" / "memory.jsonl"
        d[f"cmem:{name}"] = jl(prof)
        d[f"cmem_sha:{name}"] = sha(prof)
    d["wmem"] = jl(fr / f"U2-WALL-step1-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
    d["wmem_sha"] = sha(fr / f"U2-WALL-step1-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
    d["wlaunch"] = jl(fr / f"U2-WALL-step1-{seed}" / "launches.jsonl")
    d["wfile"] = my_sav.parse(fr / f"U2-WALL-step1-{seed}" / "current_save.sav") \
        if (fr / f"U2-WALL-step1-{seed}" / "current_save.sav").exists() else None
    d["wpurity"] = json.loads((rn / f"U2-WALL-{seed}" / "step1_run" / "purity.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) \
        if (rn / f"U2-WALL-{seed}" / "step1_run" / "purity.json").exists() else None
    d["wworld"] = rn / f"U2-WALL-{seed}" / "step1_run" / "world.jsonl"
    d["wrun"] = rn / f"U2-WALL-{seed}" / "step1_run"
    for name in ("CHAIN", "CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD"):
        d[f"chmem:{name}"] = jl(fr / f"{name}-B-{seed}" / "memory.jsonl")
        r = row(out, name, seed)  # the DRIVER's flag (not recomputed): top level, or in its grade
        d[f"chcounted:{name}"] = r["counted"] if "counted" in r else r.get("grade", {}).get("counted")
    for name in ("R2-RETRY", "R2-RETRY-OLD"):
        d[f"res:{name}"] = result(rn / f"{name}-{seed}" / "child_b.out")
    for name in ("K-INSIDE-OLD", "K-SILENT-OLD"):
        d[f"res:{name}"] = result(rn / f"{name}-{seed}" / "child_b.out")
    return d


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- checks
def truth_of(d, counter):
    for c, t in d["truth_kafter"]:
        if c == counter:
            return t
    return None


def same_content(loaded, truth) -> bool:
    return bool(loaded and truth) and loaded["pids"] == list(truth["pids"]) and loaded["map"] == list(truth["map"]) \
        and loaded["pos"] == list(truth["pos"]) and loaded["play_time"] == list(truth["play_time"])


def u2_budget(d) -> dict:
    c, notes = {}, {}
    s2 = truth_of(d, 2)
    kf = d["file:K-AFTER"]
    c["R1_kafter_file_is_s2"] = bool(kf) and kf["verdict"] == "S2" and same_content(kf["loaded"], s2)
    lines = [l for l in d["llaunch:LAUNCH"] if l.get("kind") == "launch_end"]
    ll = lines[-1] if lines else {}
    notes["launch_line"] = {k: ll.get(k) for k in ("exit_reason", "exit_code", "verdict", "verdict_code", "frames",
                                                    "real_frames", "hard_kill")}
    pur = d["lpurity"] or {}
    c["R2_launch_line_budget_stop"] = (str(ll.get("exit_reason", "")).startswith(
        f"play: stopped: not finished within {BUDGET} frames") and ll.get("exit_code") == 0
        and ll.get("verdict_code") == 0 and ll.get("verdict") == "CLEAN"
        and ll.get("real_frames") == ll.get("frames") == BUDGET)
    c["R2b_purity_agrees"] = (pur.get("counters", {}).get("real_frames") == ll.get("real_frames")
                              and pur.get("verdict") == "CLEAN" and pur.get("facts", {}).get("exit_code") == 0)
    mem = d["lmem:LAUNCH"]
    s2_ids = save_by_identity(mem, s2) if s2 else []
    notes["s2_ids"] = s2_ids
    run = str(d["lrun:LAUNCH"])
    sess = [s for s in sessions(mem) if Path(str(s.get("run_dir"))) == Path(run)]
    ls = sess[-1] if sess else {}
    c["R3_session_continued_s2"] = len(s2_ids) == 1 and ls.get("continued_from") == s2_ids[0]
    at = mem.index(ls) if ls in mem else len(mem)
    ends = [l for l in mem[at:] if l.get("kind") == "end" and l.get("session") == ls.get("n")]
    end = ends[-1] if ends else {}
    c["R4_end_budget_not_at_save"] = end.get("budget") is True and end.get("at_save") is False
    c["R5_cart_unwritten"] = d["lcart_sha"] is not None and d["lcart_sha"] == d["kafter_cart_sha"]
    c["R6_no_save_after_session"] = not any(l.get("kind") == "save" for l in mem[at:])
    reach = all(c.values())
    res = d["res:U2-BUDGET"] or {}
    mc = res.get("memory_check") or {}
    dr = mc.get("dropped") or {}
    words = str(mc.get("why", ""))
    c["B1_allowed"] = (mc.get("ok") is True and mc.get("end_class") == "BUDGET" and mc.get("looked_through") == 0
                       and "USER RULING 53" in words and "time or frame limit" in words)
    c["B1_set_is_launch_session"] = [e.get("n") for e in dr.get("sessions") or []] == [ls.get("n")] and \
        dr.get("save") == (s2_ids[0] if s2_ids else None)
    c["B1_host_frames_is_host_count"] = dr.get("host_frames") == ll.get("real_frames") == \
        pur.get("counters", {}).get("real_frames")
    notes["ai_frames"], notes["host_frames"] = dr.get("frames"), dr.get("host_frames")
    notes["boot_offset"] = (dr["host_frames"] - dr["frames"]) if isinstance(dr.get("frames"), int) and \
        isinstance(dr.get("host_frames"), int) else None
    c["B1_at_save_is_s2_ram_time"] = s2 is not None and list(dr.get("at_save") or []) == list(s2["play_time"][:2])
    lost_lines = sum(1 for l in mem[at:] if l.get("kind") == "lost")
    c["B1_lost_is_the_sessions_lost_lines"] = dr.get("lost") == lost_lines
    # maps: my rule = keys stood on in the session's world snapshot that are NOT in its restored_maps (the code's rule is
    # first_frame >= since); the same file (the AI's own record), a different rule
    snap = last_snapshot(Path(run) / "world.jsonl")
    restored = set(ls.get("restored_maps") or [])
    k2c = key_to_const(snap)
    mine = sorted(k2c[k] for k in (snap or {}).get("maps", {}) if k not in restored) if snap else None
    notes["maps_mine"], notes["maps_told"] = mine, dr.get("maps")
    c["B1_maps_new_to_session"] = mine is not None and sorted(dr.get("maps") or []) == mine
    notes["maps_nonempty"] = bool(mine)
    # B2
    cm = d["cmem:U2-BUDGET"]
    csess = sessions(cm)[-1] if sessions(cm) else {}
    arr, boot = res.get("arrival") or {}, res.get("boot") or {}
    c["B2_continued_s2"] = bool(s2_ids) and csess.get("continued_from") == s2_ids[0] and csess.get("how") == "continue"
    c["B2_arrival_is_s2_truth"] = s2 is not None and arr.get("pids") == list(s2["pids"]) and \
        arr.get("map") == list(s2["map"]) and arr.get("pos") == list(s2["pos"]) and \
        boot.get("play_time") == list(s2["play_time"])
    c["B2_continue_no_new_game_no_page"] = continue_pressed(res) and res.get("new_game_tasks") == [] and \
        res.get("pages") == []
    # B3 (the AI's word: a label check)
    c["B3_ai_word"] = (csess.get("dropped") or {}) == {"frames": dr.get("frames"), "lost": dr.get("lost")}
    # OLD
    ro = d["res:U2-BUDGET-OLD"] or {}
    mo = ro.get("memory_check") or {}
    c["OLD_refused_budget"] = mo.get("ok") is False and mo.get("end_class") == "BUDGET"
    c["OLD_memory_byte_identical"] = d["lmem_sha"] is not None and d["cmem_sha:U2-BUDGET-OLD"] == d["lmem_sha"]
    c["OLD_ran_u0"] = u0_tree_ran(ro)
    return {"reach": reach, "checks": c, "notes": notes}


def u0_tree_ran(res) -> bool:
    ls = (res or {}).get("launcher_sha") or {}
    path = Path(str(ls.get("file", "")))
    if not path.exists() or "U0" not in str(path):
        return False
    mine = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    if not mine.startswith(str(ls.get("sha256", "x"))):
        return False
    real = (W / "ai_player" / "host" / "launcher.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    want = real
    for old, new in U0_EDITS:
        if want.count(old) != 1:
            return False
        want = want.replace(old, new)
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == want and want != real


def u2_wall(d) -> dict:
    c, notes = {}, {}
    s2 = truth_of(d, 2)
    kf = d["file:K-AFTER"]
    c["R1_kafter_file_is_s2"] = bool(kf) and kf["verdict"] == "S2" and same_content(kf["loaded"], s2)
    lines = [l for l in d["wlaunch"] if l.get("kind") == "launch_end"]
    ll = lines[-1] if lines else {}
    pur = d["wpurity"] or {}
    notes["launch_line"] = {k: ll.get(k) for k in ("exit_reason", "exit_code", "verdict", "verdict_code", "frames",
                                                    "real_frames", "hard_kill")}
    c["R2_wall_clock_124_clean_no_hard_kill"] = (ll.get("exit_reason") == "wall-clock" and ll.get("verdict_code") == 124
                                                 and ll.get("verdict") == "CLEAN" and ll.get("hard_kill") is False)
    c["R2b_purity_agrees"] = (pur.get("facts", {}).get("exit_code") == 124 and pur.get("verdict") == "CLEAN"
                              and pur.get("counters", {}).get("real_frames") == ll.get("real_frames"))
    mem = d["wmem"]
    s2_ids = save_by_identity(mem, s2) if s2 else []
    sess = [s for s in sessions(mem) if Path(str(s.get("run_dir"))) == Path(str(d["wrun"]))]
    ls = sess[-1] if sess else {}
    c["R3_session_continued_s2"] = len(s2_ids) == 1 and ls.get("continued_from") == s2_ids[0]
    at = mem.index(ls) if ls in mem else len(mem)
    c["R4_no_end_line"] = bool(ls) and not any(l.get("kind") == "end" and l.get("session") == ls.get("n")
                                               for l in mem[at:])
    reach = all(c.values())
    res = d["res:U2-WALL"] or {}
    mc = res.get("memory_check") or {}
    dr = mc.get("dropped") or {}
    words = str(mc.get("why", ""))
    saves = [l for l in mem if l.get("kind") == "save"]
    newest = saves[-1] if saves else {}
    c["W1_allowed"] = (mc.get("ok") is True and mc.get("end_class") == "BUDGET" and "USER RULING 53" in words
                       and "time or frame limit" in words)
    c["W1_save_is_newest_line"] = dr.get("save") == newest.get("id")
    c["W1_set_is_step1_session"] = [e.get("n") for e in dr.get("sessions") or []] == [ls.get("n")]
    c["W1_ai_frames_not_recorded"] = "frames" in dr and dr.get("frames") is None
    c["W1_at_end_not_recorded"] = "at_end" in dr and dr.get("at_end") is None
    own = newest.get("id") is not None and mem.index(newest) > at  # the save was written in step 1's own session
    offset = (newest["frame"] - ls["frame_base"]) if own and isinstance(newest.get("frame"), int) and \
        isinstance(ls.get("frame_base"), int) else 0
    want = ll.get("real_frames") - offset if isinstance(ll.get("real_frames"), int) else None
    notes["host_frames"], notes["want"], notes["offset"], notes["own"] = dr.get("host_frames"), want, offset, own
    c["W1_host_frames_is_host_count"] = want is not None and dr.get("host_frames") == want
    # W2: arrival = the FILE's content (my parse); continued_from by identity
    wf = d["wfile"] or {}
    loaded = wf.get("loaded")
    arr, boot = res.get("arrival") or {}, res.get("boot") or {}
    c["W2_arrival_is_file_content"] = bool(loaded) and arr.get("pids") == loaded["pids"] and \
        arr.get("map") == loaded["map"] and arr.get("pos") == loaded["pos"] and boot.get("play_time") == loaded["play_time"]
    snap = last_snapshot(d["wworld"])
    k2c = key_to_const(snap)
    kcounter = (kf or {}).get("counter")
    rose = wf.get("counter") is not None and kcounter is not None and wf["counter"] > kcounter
    notes["counter_rose"], notes["file_counter"], notes["kafter_counter"] = rose, wf.get("counter"), kcounter
    if rose and loaded:
        const = k2c.get(f"{loaded['map'][0]}:{loaded['map'][1]}")
        matches = [l["id"] for l in saves if list(l.get("play_time") or []) == loaded["play_time"][:2]
                   and (l.get("where") or {}).get("map") == const and list((l.get("where") or {}).get("tile") or [])
                   == loaded["pos"] and [p["pid"] for p in l.get("party") or []] == loaded["pids"]]
        expect = matches[0] if len(matches) == 1 else None
        notes["identity_matches"] = matches
    else:
        expect = s2_ids[0] if len(s2_ids) == 1 else None
        notes["identity_matches"] = "S2 branch"
    cm = d["cmem:U2-WALL"]
    csess = sessions(cm)[-1] if sessions(cm) else {}
    notes["continued_from"], notes["expected"] = csess.get("continued_from"), expect
    c["W2_continued_from_by_identity"] = expect is not None and csess.get("continued_from") == expect
    c["W2_continue_no_new_game_no_page"] = continue_pressed(res) and res.get("new_game_tasks") == [] and \
        res.get("pages") == []
    ro = d["res:U2-WALL-OLD"] or {}
    mo = ro.get("memory_check") or {}
    c["OLD_refused_budget"] = mo.get("ok") is False and mo.get("end_class") == "BUDGET"
    c["OLD_memory_byte_identical"] = d["wmem_sha"] is not None and d["cmem_sha:U2-WALL-OLD"] == d["wmem_sha"]
    c["OLD_ran_u0"] = u0_tree_ran(ro)
    notes["saves_in_step1"] = [(l["id"], l.get("play_time"), (l.get("where") or {}).get("map")) for l in saves
                               if mem.index(l) > at]
    return {"reach": reach, "checks": c, "notes": notes}


def bcont(mem):
    s = sessions(mem)
    return s[-1].get("continued_from") if s and s[-1].get("how") == "continue" else None


def brestored(mem):
    s = sessions(mem)
    return sorted(s[-1].get("restored_maps") or []) if s else None


def s1_frame(mem):
    for l in mem:
        if l.get("kind") == "save" and l.get("id") == 1:
            return l.get("frame")
    return None


def discriminators(d) -> list:
    out = []
    for twin in ("K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT"):
        f1 = s1_frame(d[f"amem:{twin}"])
        new = new_maps_after(d[f"aworld:{twin}"], f1) if f1 is not None else None
        rn, ro = brestored(d[f"bmem:{twin}"]), brestored(d[f"bmem:{twin}-OLD"])
        vals = [bcont(d[f"bmem:{twin}"]), bcont(d[f"bmem:{twin}-OLD"])]
        inter = [sorted(set(new or []) & set(rn or [])), sorted(set(new or []) & set(ro or []))]
        out.append({"pair": f"{twin} / {twin}-OLD", "NEW": new, "NEW maps restored": inter, "continued_from": vals,
                    "differ": vals[0] != vals[1] and inter[0] != inter[1]})
    fa, fi = d["file:K-AFTER"], d["file:K-INSIDE"]
    vals = [bcont(d["bmem:K-AFTER"]), bcont(d["bmem:K-INSIDE"])]
    files = [fa and fa["verdict"], fi and fi["verdict"]]
    out.append({"pair": "K-AFTER / K-INSIDE", "FILE": files, "continued_from": vals,
                "differ": files[0] != files[1] and vals[0] != vals[1]})
    looks = []
    for name in ("LAUNCH", "LAUNCH-OLD"):
        mem = d[f"lmem:{name}"]
        sess = [s for s in sessions(mem) if Path(str(s.get("run_dir"))) == Path(str(d[f"lrun:{name}"]))]
        base = sess[-1].get("frame_base") if sess else None
        looks.append(first_look(d[f"lactions:{name}"], base) if isinstance(base, int) else None)
    out.append({"pair": "LAUNCH / LAUNCH-OLD", "first look (state, emulator frame)": looks,
                "differ": looks[0] != looks[1]})
    files = [d["file:K-FIRST"] and d["file:K-FIRST"]["verdict"], d["file:CONTROL"] and d["file:CONTROL"]["verdict"]]
    out.append({"pair": "K-FIRST / CONTROL", "FILE": files, "differ": files[0] != files[1]})
    f1 = s1_frame(d["amem:K-BETWEEN"])
    new = new_maps_after(d["aworld:K-BETWEEN"], f1) if f1 is not None else None
    rest = brestored(d["bmem:K-BETWEEN"])
    out.append({"pair": "K-BETWEEN (NEW at the kill vs the restored memory)", "NEW": new, "restored_maps": rest,
                "differ": bool(new) and not (set(new) & set(rest or []))})
    own = {}
    for name in ("CHAIN", "CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD"):
        mem = d[f"chmem:{name}"]
        ss = sessions(mem)
        at = mem.index(ss[-1]) if ss else len(mem)
        bsaves = [l for l in mem[at:] if l.get("kind") == "save"]
        first = bsaves[0] if bsaves else None
        own[name] = {"id": first and first["id"], "parent": first and first.get("parent"),
                     "last_save_id": rebuild_last_save_id(mem, first["id"]) if first else None,
                     "counted": d[f"chcounted:{name}"]}
    both = bool(own["CHAIN"]["counted"]) and bool(own["CHAIN-OLD"]["counted"])
    vals = [own["CHAIN"]["parent"], own["CHAIN-OLD"]["parent"]]
    out.append({"pair": "CHAIN / CHAIN-OLD (B's save line's parent)", "values": vals, "own_id": own["CHAIN"]["id"],
                "both_counted (driver's flag)": both, "differ": both and vals[0] != vals[1]})
    both = bool(own["CHAIN"]["counted"]) and bool(own["CHAIN-R1-OLD"]["counted"])
    vals = [own["CHAIN"]["last_save_id"], own["CHAIN-R1-OLD"]["last_save_id"]]
    out.append({"pair": "CHAIN / CHAIN-R1-OLD (the rebuilt goals.last_save.id)", "values": vals,
                "own_id": own["CHAIN"]["id"], "both_counted (driver's flag)": both,
                "differ": both and vals[0] != vals[1]})
    for pair, a, b in (("R2-RETRY / R2-RETRY-OLD (memory_check.ok)", "R2-RETRY", "R2-RETRY-OLD"),
                       ("U2-BUDGET / U2-BUDGET-OLD (memory_check.ok)", "U2-BUDGET", "U2-BUDGET-OLD"),
                       ("U2-WALL / U2-WALL-OLD (memory_check.ok)", "U2-WALL", "U2-WALL-OLD")):
        ma = (d[f"res:{a}"] or {}).get("memory_check") or {}
        mb = (d[f"res:{b}"] or {}).get("memory_check") or {}
        vals = [ma.get("ok"), mb.get("ok")]
        out.append({"pair": pair, "values": vals, "classes": [ma.get("end_class"), mb.get("end_class")],
                    "differ": vals == [True, False]})
    return out


def determinism(d) -> list:
    """MY determinism line: each killed arm's A-run decisions (frame, decision, action, reason) up to its own last
    whole line equal CONTROL's at the same frames (one trajectory, replayed; NOT a replication)"""
    def seq(path):
        # the reason text carries the look's wall-time cost ("cost=1.60ms"), which is not game state: dropped
        return [(l.get("frame"), l.get("decision"), l.get("action"), COST.sub("", str(l.get("reason"))))
                for l in jl(path) if isinstance(l.get("frame"), int)]
    ctl = seq(d["aactions:CONTROL"])
    out = []
    for arm in ("K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST", "K-BETWEEN", "K-BETWEEN-MID"):
        mine = seq(d[f"aactions:{arm}"])
        if not mine:
            out.append({"arm": arm, "lines": 0, "same_as_control": None})
            continue
        last = mine[-1][0]
        ref = [x for x in ctl if x[0] <= last]
        k = min(len(mine), len(ref))
        # the arm's last line may be cut by the kill; compare every whole line up to the shorter of the two
        same = mine[:k] == ref[:k] and abs(len(mine) - len(ref)) <= 1
        out.append({"arm": arm, "lines": len(mine), "to_frame": last, "same_as_control": same})
    return out


FAULTS = {
    "budget_real_frames_short": "LAUNCH's launch line real_frames 2619 (the host count did not reach the budget)",
    "drop_host_frames_self_graded": "U2-BUDGET's dropped.host_frames replaced by the AI's frames + 1",
    "drop_maps_extra": "U2-BUDGET's dropped.maps gains a map the session never stood on",
    "drop_ignored": "U2-BUDGET's memory_check refused (ok False)",
    "wall_no_code": "U2-WALL step 1's launch line verdict_code 0",
    "wall_stamp_only": "U2-WALL's FILE party PID changed (stamp and tile still match)",
    "old_memory_changed": "U2-BUDGET-OLD's memory after differs from the frozen memory",
    "old_not_u0": "U2-WALL-OLD's launcher_sha points at the REAL launcher.py",
    "kafter_cart_sector_broken": "K-AFTER's cart: one sector of the S2 slot gets a bad checksum (my parser must see it)",
    "arrival_shifted": "U2-BUDGET's arrival tile shifted by one",
    "disc_old_equal": "U2-WALL-OLD's memory_check.ok set True (the discriminator must stop differing)",
    "disc_chain_parent_equal": "CHAIN-OLD's B save line parent set to CHAIN's (the discriminator must stop differing)",
    "disc_kinside_old_like_new": "K-INSIDE-OLD's B memory replaced by K-INSIDE's (the OLD continued the same save)",
    "det_one_press_changed": "one decision of K-SILENT's A run, early in its record, changed (one trajectory broken)",
}


def plant(d, name):
    d = copy.deepcopy(d)
    if name == "budget_real_frames_short":
        for l in d["llaunch:LAUNCH"]:
            if l.get("kind") == "launch_end":
                l["real_frames"] = BUDGET - 1
    elif name == "drop_host_frames_self_graded":
        dr = d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["memory_check"]["dropped"]
        dr["host_frames"] = (dr.get("frames") or 0) + 1
    elif name == "drop_maps_extra":
        d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["memory_check"]["dropped"]["maps"] = \
            list(d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["memory_check"]["dropped"].get("maps") or []) + ["PETALBURG_CITY"]
    elif name == "drop_ignored":
        d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["memory_check"]["ok"] = False
    elif name == "wall_no_code":
        for l in d["wlaunch"]:
            if l.get("kind") == "launch_end":
                l["verdict_code"] = 0
    elif name == "wall_stamp_only":
        d["wfile"]["loaded"]["pids"] = ["deadbeef"]
    elif name == "old_memory_changed":
        d["cmem_sha:U2-BUDGET-OLD"] = "0" * 64
    elif name == "old_not_u0":
        d["res:U2-WALL-OLD"]["launcher_sha"] = {"file": str(W / "ai_player" / "host" / "launcher.py"),
                                                "sha256": "x"}
    elif name == "kafter_cart_sector_broken":
        out = Path(d["_out"])
        blob = bytearray((out / "frozen" / f"K-AFTER-{d['seed']}" / "current_save.sav").read_bytes())
        parsed = my_sav.parse_bytes(bytes(blob))
        slot = parsed.get("loaded_slot", 0)
        blob[slot * 14 * 0x1000 + 0xF6] ^= 0xFF  # a byte inside the loaded slot's first sector data (0x1000 slot base)
        d["file:K-AFTER"] = my_sav.parse_bytes(bytes(blob))
    elif name == "arrival_shifted":
        d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["arrival"]["pos"] = [d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["arrival"]["pos"][0] + 1,
                                                d["res:U2-BUDGET"]["arrival"]["pos"][1]]
    elif name == "disc_old_equal":
        d["res:U2-WALL-OLD"]["memory_check"]["ok"] = True
    elif name == "disc_chain_parent_equal":
        mem = d["chmem:CHAIN-OLD"]
        ref = d["chmem:CHAIN"]
        ss, rs = sessions(mem), sessions(ref)
        if not ss or not rs:
            return d  # a seed with no B record (CHAIN did not run its B): nothing to plant there
        a = mem.index(ss[-1])
        b = ref.index(rs[-1])
        mine = [l for l in mem[a:] if l.get("kind") == "save"]
        theirs = [l for l in ref[b:] if l.get("kind") == "save"]
        if mine and theirs:
            mine[0]["parent"] = theirs[0].get("parent")
    elif name == "disc_kinside_old_like_new":
        d["bmem:K-INSIDE-OLD"] = copy.deepcopy(d["bmem:K-INSIDE"])
    elif name == "det_one_press_changed":
        src = Path(d["aactions:K-SILENT"])
        lines = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        rec = json.loads(lines[300])
        rec["action"] = str(rec.get("action")) + " (PLANTED)"
        lines[300] = json.dumps(rec)
        plant_dir = HERE / "_fault_tmp"
        plant_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        dst = plant_dir / f"actions-K-SILENT-{d['seed']}.jsonl"
        dst.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        d["aactions:K-SILENT"] = dst
    return d


def run(out, seeds, fault=None) -> dict:
    report = {}
    for seed in seeds:
        d = load(out, seed)
        d["_out"] = str(out)
        if fault:
            d = plant(d, fault)
        report[seed] = {"U2-BUDGET": u2_budget(d), "U2-WALL": u2_wall(d), "discriminators": discriminators(d),
                        "determinism": determinism(d)}
    return report


def flat(report) -> dict:
    """every check and discriminator 'differ' as one flat {name: bool}"""
    f = {}
    for seed, r in report.items():
        for arm in ("U2-BUDGET", "U2-WALL"):
            f[f"{seed} {arm} reach"] = r[arm]["reach"]
            for k, v in r[arm]["checks"].items():
                f[f"{seed} {arm} {k}"] = v
        for x in r["discriminators"]:
            f[f"{seed} DISC {x['pair']}"] = x["differ"]
        for x in r["determinism"]:
            f[f"{seed} DET {x['arm']}"] = x["same_as_control"]
    return f


def main() -> int:
    out = Path(sys.argv[1])
    seeds = range(int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3]) + 1)
    args = sys.argv[4:]
    jfile = args[args.index("--json") + 1] if "--json" in args else None
    if "--fault" in args:
        name = args[args.index("--fault") + 1]
        clean, bad = flat(run(out, seeds)), flat(run(out, seeds, name))
        flipped = [k for k in clean if clean[k] is True and bad.get(k) is not True]
        print(f"FAULT {name} ({FAULTS[name]}): {len(flipped)} check(s) flipped True -> False: {flipped[:8]}")
        return 0 if flipped else 1
    report = run(out, seeds)
    if jfile:
        Path(jfile).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    for seed, r in report.items():
        for arm in ("U2-BUDGET", "U2-WALL"):
            bad = [k for k, v in r[arm]["checks"].items() if v is not True]
            print(f"MY {arm} {seed}: reach {r[arm]['reach']}; checks {sum(v is True for v in r[arm]['checks'].values())}"
                  f" of {len(r[arm]['checks'])} true; not true: {bad or 'none'}")
            print(f"   notes {json.dumps(r[arm]['notes'], default=str)[:900]}")
        for x in r["discriminators"]:
            print(f"MY DISCRIMINATOR {seed} {json.dumps(x, default=str)}")
        for x in r["determinism"]:
            print(f"MY DETERMINISM {seed} {json.dumps(x)}")
    fl = flat(report)
    u2_false = [k for k, v in fl.items() if "DISC" not in k and v is not True]
    disc = [k for k in fl if "DISC" in k]
    det = [x for r in report.values() for x in r["determinism"]]
    print(f"MY U2 CHECKS: {sum(1 for k, v in fl.items() if 'DISC' not in k and v is True)} of "
          f"{sum(1 for k in fl if 'DISC' not in k)} true; not true: {u2_false or 'none'}")
    print(f"MY DISCRIMINATORS: {sum(fl[k] is True for k in disc)} of {len(disc)} differ; "
          f"not differing: {[k for k in disc if fl[k] is not True] or 'none'}")
    print(f"MY DETERMINISM: {sum(x['same_as_control'] is True for x in det)} of {len(det)} same as CONTROL")
    if "--all-faults" in args:
        ok = 0
        for name in FAULTS:
            bad = flat(run(out, seeds, name))
            flipped = [k for k in fl if fl[k] is True and bad.get(k) is not True]
            ok += bool(flipped)
            print(f"FAULT {name}: {'FAILS AS IT MUST' if flipped else 'NOT SEEN'} ({len(flipped)} flipped, "
                  f"e.g. {flipped[:3]})")
        print(f"MY INSTRUMENT FAULTS: {ok} of {len(FAULTS)} planted faults seen")
        return 0 if ok == len(FAULTS) else 1
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

######## 21. The verifier's instrument: my_sav.py

==== The verifier's instrument: my_sav.py: my_sav.py (sha256 f8571b6caca4e4d86ae83a63297d313f9d07d594d7470660d04cd6d3691c407d, 3983 bytes) ====
"""(U2a VERIFICATION) MY OWN Emerald save-file parser, written from the game's save format, importing nothing of
ai_player or the driver. It is the second path for every FILE check.

Format (pokeemerald `save.h` / `save.c`): 32 sectors of 0x1000 bytes; slot A = physical sectors 0-13, slot B = 14-27.
Each sector's footer: 0xFF4 id (u16), 0xFF6 checksum (u16), 0xFF8 signature (u32 0x08012025), 0xFFC counter (u32).
Checksum = 32-bit sum of the section's u32 words over its size, folded (hi16 + lo16) & 0xFFFF.
A slot is OK when all 14 ids are present with good signature and checksum (the game's GetSaveValidStatus); EMPTY when no
sector carries the signature; otherwise ERROR. The game loads the higher-counter OK slot; if the other slot is ERROR it
shows "The save file is corrupted. The previous save file will be loaded." (my verdict "S<c>+ERROR").
SaveBlock2 (section 0): playTimeHours u16 @0x0E, minutes @0x10, seconds @0x11, vblanks @0x12.
SaveBlock1 (section 1): pos x,y s16 @0x00/0x02; location mapGroup s8 @0x04, mapNum s8 @0x05;
playerPartyCount u8 @0x234; playerParty @0x238, 100 bytes each, personality u32 first.

    py -3.12 my_sav.py FILE...      prints my parse of each
"""
import json
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SIG = 0x08012025
SIZES = [3884, 3968, 3968, 3968, 3848] + [3968] * 8 + [2000]


def checksum(data: bytes, size: int) -> int:
    total = sum(struct.unpack_from(f"<{size // 4}I", data, 0)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return ((total >> 16) + total) & 0xFFFF


def sectors(blob: bytes, slot: int) -> dict:
    out = {"good": {}, "signed": 0, "counters": set()}
    for phys in range(slot * 14, slot * 14 + 14):
        sec = blob[phys * 0x1000:(phys + 1) * 0x1000]
        if len(sec) < 0x1000:
            continue
        sid, chk, sig, counter = struct.unpack_from("<HHII", sec, 0xFF4)
        if sig != SIG:
            continue
        out["signed"] += 1
        if sid < 14 and checksum(sec, SIZES[sid]) == chk:
            out["good"][sid] = (sec, counter)
            out["counters"].add(counter)
    return out


def slot_status(info: dict) -> str:
    if info["signed"] == 0:
        return "EMPTY"
    if len(info["good"]) == 14 and len(info["counters"]) == 1:
        return "OK"
    return "ERROR"


def content(info: dict) -> dict:
    s0 = info["good"][0][0]
    s1 = info["good"][1][0]
    hours, minutes, seconds, vbl = struct.unpack_from("<HBBB", s0, 0x0E)
    x, y = struct.unpack_from("<hh", s1, 0)
    group, num = struct.unpack_from("<bb", s1, 4)
    count = s1[0x234]
    pids = [f"{struct.unpack_from('<I', s1, 0x238 + 100 * i)[0]:08x}" for i in range(min(count, 6))]
    return {"play_time": [hours, minutes, seconds, vbl], "map": [group, num], "pos": [x, y], "pids": pids}


def parse_bytes(blob: bytes) -> dict:
    slots = []
    for slot in (0, 1):
        info = sectors(blob, slot)
        status = slot_status(info)
        counter = next(iter(info["counters"])) if status == "OK" else None
        slots.append({"slot": slot, "status": status, "counter": counter, "good": len(info["good"]),
                      "signed": info["signed"], "info": info})
    ok = [s for s in slots if s["status"] == "OK"]
    if not ok:
        verdict = "EMPTY" if all(s["status"] == "EMPTY" for s in slots) else "CORRUPT"
        return {"verdict": verdict, "counter": None, "loaded": None,
                "slots": [{k: v for k, v in s.items() if k != "info"} for s in slots]}
    best = max(ok, key=lambda s: s["counter"])
    other = slots[1 - best["slot"]]
    verdict = f"S{best['counter']}" + ("+ERROR" if other["status"] == "ERROR" else "")
    return {"verdict": verdict, "counter": best["counter"], "loaded_slot": best["slot"],
            "loaded": content(best["info"]), "slots": [{k: v for k, v in s.items() if k != "info"} for s in slots]}


def parse(path) -> dict:
    return parse_bytes(Path(path).read_bytes())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for f in sys.argv[1:]:
        print(f, json.dumps(parse(f)))

######## 26. The comparison script

==== The comparison script: compare.py (sha256 5948b3722529d56406782fc81cd6233a2f88171656cbaa142d544e93d6e86e5a, 3042 bytes) ====
"""Compare MY discriminators / determinism (my_checks.py output) with the DRIVER's (live_r1 verdict output), value by
value, per seed and pair.   py -3.12 compare.py MY_OUT DRIVER_VERDICT_OUT"""
import json
import sys

mine_txt, drv_txt = (open(p, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines() for p in sys.argv[1:3])
KEYS = {"K-INSIDE / K-INSIDE-OLD": ["NEW", "NEW maps restored", "continued_from", "differ"],
        "K-SILENT / K-SILENT-OLD": ["NEW", "NEW maps restored", "continued_from", "differ"],
        "K-AFTER / K-INSIDE": ["FILE", "continued_from", "differ"],
        "LAUNCH / LAUNCH-OLD": ["first look (state, emulator frame)", "differ"],
        "K-FIRST / CONTROL": ["FILE", "differ"],
        "K-BETWEEN": ["NEW", "restored_maps", "differ"],
        "CHAIN / CHAIN-OLD": ["values", "own_id", "differ"],
        "CHAIN / CHAIN-R1-OLD": ["values", "own_id", "differ"],
        "R2-RETRY / R2-RETRY-OLD": ["values", "classes", "differ"],
        "U2-BUDGET / U2-BUDGET-OLD": ["values", "classes", "differ"],
        "U2-WALL / U2-WALL-OLD": ["values", "classes", "differ"]}


def short(pair):
    for k in KEYS:
        if pair.startswith(k):
            return k
    return None


mine, drv = {}, {}
for line in mine_txt:
    if line.startswith("MY DISCRIMINATOR "):
        seed, _, js = line[len("MY DISCRIMINATOR "):].partition(" ")
        d = json.loads(js)
        mine[(int(seed), short(d["pair"]))] = d
for line in drv_txt:
    if line.startswith("DISCRIMINATOR "):
        d = json.loads(line[len("DISCRIMINATOR "):])
        drv[(d["seed"], short(d["pair"]))] = d
agree = disagree = 0
for key in sorted(set(mine) | set(drv), key=str):
    m, r = mine.get(key), drv.get(key)
    if m is None or r is None:
        print(f"MISSING {key}: mine {m is not None}, driver {r is not None}")
        disagree += 1
        continue
    diffs = [k for k in KEYS[key[1]] if m.get(k) != r.get(k)]
    if diffs:
        disagree += 1
        print(f"DISAGREE {key}: " + "; ".join(f"{k}: mine {m.get(k)} driver {r.get(k)}" for k in diffs))
    else:
        agree += 1
        print(f"AGREE {key}: " + "; ".join(f"{k}={json.dumps(m.get(k))}" for k in KEYS[key[1]]))
det_m = {}
for line in mine_txt:
    if line.startswith("MY DETERMINISM "):
        seed, _, js = line[len("MY DETERMINISM "):].partition(" ")
        d = json.loads(js)
        det_m[(int(seed), d["arm"])] = d["same_as_control"]
det_r = {}
for line in drv_txt:
    if line.startswith("DETERMINISM "):
        d = json.loads(line[len("DETERMINISM "):])
        det_r[(d["seed"], d["arm"])] = d["same_as_control"]
det_agree = sum(1 for k in det_r if det_m.get(k) == det_r[k])
print(f"DETERMINISM: mine {sum(v is True for v in det_m.values())} of {len(det_m)} same; driver "
      f"{sum(v is True for v in det_r.values())} of {len(det_r)}; agree on {det_agree} of {len(det_r)} "
      f"(arms: mine {sorted(set(a for _, a in det_m))}, driver {sorted(set(a for _, a in det_r))})")
print(f"DISCRIMINATOR VALUES: agree {agree} of {agree + disagree} pairs; disagree {disagree}")
